- **biochem-organic-chemistry-and-analytical-methods**: Use when working with organic structures or interpreting an analytical result: structure, functional groups, stereochemistry and isomerism, and reaction mechanism as the reasoning that actually transfers rather than a list of named reactions; plus analytical methods and spectroscopy — chromatography, mass spectrometry, NMR, IR and UV-Vis — and what each one can and cannot tell you.
